CMPXCHG8B_LOCK (M64) - Latency

Operands

  • Operand 1 (r/w): Memory
  • Operand 2 (r/w, suppressed): Register (EDX)
  • Operand 3 (r/w, suppressed): Register (EAX)
  • Operand 4 (r, suppressed): Register (ECX)
  • Operand 5 (r, suppressed): Register (EBX)
  • Operand 6 (w, suppressed): Flags (ZF: w)

Latency operand 1 → 1 (address, base register): ≤20

Latency operand 1 → 1 (address, index register): ≤20

Latency operand 1 → 1 (memory): 13

Latency operand 1 → 2 (address, base register): 17

Latency operand 1 → 2 (address, index register): 17

Latency operand 1 → 2 (memory): ≤14

Latency operand 1 → 3 (address, base register): 16

Latency operand 1 → 3 (address, index register): 16

Latency operand 1 → 3 (memory): ≤14

Latency operand 1 → 6 (address, base register): 15

Latency operand 1 → 6 (address, index register): 15

Latency operand 1 → 6 (memory): ≤14

Latency operand 2 → 1: ≤16

Latency operand 2 → 2: 13

Latency operand 2 → 3: 13

Latency operand 2 → 6: 14

Latency operand 3 → 1: ≤16

Latency operand 3 → 2: 13

Latency operand 3 → 3: 13

Latency operand 3 → 6: 14

Latency operand 4 → 1: ≤16

Latency operand 4 → 2: 14

Latency operand 4 → 3: 14

Latency operand 4 → 6: 15

Latency operand 5 → 1: ≤16

Latency operand 5 → 2: 14

Latency operand 5 → 3: 14

Latency operand 5 → 6: 15

Latency operand 1 → 1 (address, base register): ≤20
